  17: 
  18: The 1h/5m blocks in all three paths below must be replaced as a whole by one
  19: shared assessment call. Do not add this decision to `FinalDecisionService`.
  20: 
  21: 1. `TradeExecutionValidationService.validateBuy()` ƒ?" direct 1m BUYs.
  22: 2. `TradeExecutionValidationService.validateBuyContext()` ƒ?" accumulated/setup
  23:    authority, whose reference may be a 5m signal.
  24: 3. `OpportunityConsolidationService.evaluate()` ƒ?" consolidated BUYs.
  25: 
  26: Each replacement owns the current lookup, freshness, 5m veto, and 1h veto
  27: block in that method. The remaining profile/consolidation logic consumes the
  28: assessment's effective context; it must not reread the raw 1h decision as an
  29: independent veto.
  30: 
  31: ## Immutable signal evidence
  32: 
  33: Migration `V107` adds:
  34: 
  35: ```sql
  36: ALTER TABLE trade_signal ADD COLUMN data_quality_valid BOOLEAN NULL;
  37: ```
  38: 
  39: `AnalysisService` writes `MarketContextSnapshot.dataValid()` through the
  40: existing `TradeSignal.builder()` call for every newly generated signal on every
  41: interval. Historic rows are never backfilled: `NULL` means unknown/invalid.
  42: 
  43: FIX-154 does not depend on `signal_mode` or any other FIX-153 runtime/schema
  44: feature. `data_quality_valid = true` is sufficient evidence eligibility for
  45: this release; false or `NULL` is unusable. Historical-only exclusion is added
  46: later with FIX-153, after its own migration/runtime release is approved.
  47: 
  48: Historical `btcContextEntryAllowed=false` is audit evidence only. It is never a
  49: FIX-154 usability or hard-veto predicate. The current 1m BTC guard is unchanged.
  50: 
  51: ## Time-bounded selection and fixed freshness
  52: 
  53: For a request with reference signal `R`, use `R.generatedAt` as `evaluatedAt`.
  54: Select the latest same-symbol 5m and 1h signal such that both its generated
  55: time and its candle close are at or before `evaluatedAt`; no wall-clock lookup
  56: is permitted. Replay supplies the same historical reference time.
  57: 
  58: Existing fixed freshness limits remain authoritative:
  59: 
  60: | Interval | Maximum age |
  61: |---|---:|
  62: | 5m | 20 minutes |
  63: | 1h | 3 hours |
  64: 
  65: `MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION` and `MISSING_1H_CONTEXT` retain their existing
  66: meaning and priority when their respective interval is missing or stale. A
  67: stale signal is not quietly converted into an allowed BUY just because it is
  68: `NOT_TRUSTED` for FIX-154.
  69: 
  70: ## Usability and 1h strength
  71: 
  72: A fresh row is usable for FIX-154 only if `data_quality_valid = true`.
  73: 
  74: FIX-154 v1 does not invent ADX/DMI/VWAP/CMF evidence, because those indicators
  75: are not currently persisted. Its fixed strength definition is:
  76: 
  77: ```text
  78: HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE = 1h decision == STRONG_SELL
  79:                          AND confidence_score >= 70
  80: SOFT_CONFLICT          = fresh, quality-valid 1h decision == SELL
  81:                          OR STRONG_SELL with confidence_score below 70
  82: NOT_TRUSTED            = fresh but quality-invalid/unknown/historical-only 1h
  83:                          OR fresh non-bearish 1h
  84: ```
  85: 
  86: `confidence_score` is the persisted effective confidence. `raw_confidence_score`
  87: is explicitly not used. The constant `70` is a named FIX-154 configuration
  88: default, persisted in the audit row, and may change only through a reviewed
  89: strategy change with Replay regression.
  90: 
  91: ## Exact 1h A- 5m decision matrix
  92: 
  93: First apply existing missing/stale checks. Only then apply this table.
  94: 
  95: | 1h state | 5m state | Final HTF result before profile | Profile inputs |
  96: |---|---|---|---|
  97: | missing/stale | any | existing `MISSING_1H_CONTEXT` rejection | none |
  98: | any | missing/stale | existing `MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION` rejection | none |
  99: | NOT_TRUSTED bearish from false/NULL quality | bearish | legacy `5M_BEARISH_VETO` | none |
 100: | NOT_TRUSTED bearish from false/NULL quality | bullish/neutral/watch | `1H_BEARISH_VETO` | none |
 101: | NOT_TRUSTED non-bearish from false/NULL quality | bearish | legacy `5M_BEARISH_VETO` | none |
 102: | NOT_TRUSTED non-bearish from false/NULL quality | bullish/neutral/watch | continue to profile | raw 1h, raw 5m |
 103: | SOFT_CONFLICT | bearish | legacy `5M_BEARISH_VETO` | none |
 104: | SOFT_CONFLICT | bullish/neutral/watch | continue to profile, cap resulting first entry at 25% | effective 1h=`NEUTRAL`, raw 5m |
 105: | HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE | bearish, quality-valid | legacy `5M_BEARISH_VETO`; audit `HARD_CONFIRMATION_PRESENT` | none |
 106: | HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE | bullish/neutral/watch, quality-valid | `HTF_TRUSTED_BEARISH_VETO` | none |
 107: | fresh, quality-valid non-bearish 1h | non-bearish | continue to profile | raw 1h, raw 5m |
 108: 
 109: The profile call produces the final decision for every `continue to profile`
 110: row. It may still return its existing insufficient-confirmation code. It must
 111: not consult the raw 1h row after this mapping.
 112: 
 113: `5m quality=false/NULL` does not alter the legacy 5m fallback. A fresh raw
 114: bearish 5m still returns `5M_BEARISH_VETO`, exactly as today. It merely cannot
 115: act as new FIX-154 hard-veto confirmation or a soft-conflict release.
 116: 
 117: The veto order is fixed: a bearish 5m returns `5M_BEARISH_VETO` before any
 118: trusted-1h result. If the 1h is hard, audit records `HARD_CONFIRMATION_PRESENT`
 119: on that legacy outcome. When 5m is not bearish, a hard trusted 1h returns
 120: `HTF_TRUSTED_BEARISH_VETO`. The only deliberate lasting relaxation is a trusted
 121: SOFT bearish 1h: an otherwise allowed initial entry is capped at 25%.
 122: 
 123: ## Profile contract
 124: 
 125: `conservativeBuy`, `balancedBuy`, `balancedBuyWithNeutralFiveException`, and
 126: `aggressiveBuy` receive the assessment's effective 1h/5m contextƒ?"not raw
 127: selected 1h evidence. A SOFT conflict maps to `NEUTRAL`, never `WATCH`, then
 128: the validation result is capped with `min(profilePositionPercent, 25)`.
 129: This means the neutral-5m/direct-BUY exception still requires a genuinely
 130: bullish 1h and cannot be unlocked by SOFT evidence. An untrusted non-bearish
 131: 1h retains its raw decision so deployment does not accidentally tighten current
 132: bullish authority.
 133: 
 134: The assessment result carries `maxInitialPercent`: `25` for `SOFT_CONFLICT`,
 135: otherwise no cap. Every caller that selects a size must use
 136: `min(itsCalculatedPercent, maxInitialPercent)` when the cap is present. This
 137: includes direct validation, `validateBuyContext()` callers (the 5m-trigger
 138: path and accumulated evidence), and
 139: `OpportunityConsolidationService.consolidatedPositionPercent()`.
 140: 
 141: The raw selected signals remain audit evidence. Existing 5m requirements inside
 142: profiles remain unchanged. HARD and untrusted-bearish outcomes never call a
 143: profile method.
 144: 
 145: ### Add-on contract
 146: 
 147: `ExecutionIntelligenceService.progressivePositionDecision()` already reads raw
 148: 1h evidence and requires `WATCH` or bullish support for `SCOUT_ENTRY`,
 149: `CONFIRMATION_ADD`, and `PROGRESSIVE_TREND_ADD`. Keep that check unchanged and
 150: outside the three replacement paths; it already blocks HARD, SOFT, and
 151: untrusted-bearish 1h states. Do not route it through effective FIX-154 context,
 152: which could widen behavior. When that existing check rejects while the assessed
 153: band is SOFT, record `SOFT_HTF_ADD_BLOCKED` as audit/metric evidence only.
 154: 
 155: ## Append-only execution audit
 156: 
 157: FIX-154 is evaluated during execution/consolidation, not while a signal is
 158: saved. Migration `V107` creates append-only `buy_htf_assessment_audit`:
 159: 
 160: - primary key ID;
 161: - reference signal ID, evaluation path (`DIRECT_BUY`, `BUY_CONTEXT`,
 162:   `CONSOLIDATION`, `PROGRESSIVE_TREND_ADD`, `CONFIRMATION_ADD`,
 163:   `ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE_ADD`) and `evaluated_at`; no unique constraint;
 164: - raw/effective 1h and 5m signal IDs, decisions, signal modes, candle closes,
 165:   ages, data-quality values, and usability reasons;
 166: - configured confidence threshold, band, legacy-veto result, profile context,
 167:   final result/reason, Production/Replay mode, and creation timestamp.
 168: 
 169: The audit is written best-effort after the relevant execution/consolidation
 170: decision is known. A duplicate or persistence failure is logged and increments
 171: `fix154.audit_write_failure_count`; it must never roll back, reject, or alter a
 172: trading decision. It is evidence onlyƒ?"not a fence or an input to later trading.
 173: 
 174: ## Deployment behavior
 175: 
 176: Immediately after deployment all pre-V107 1h/5m rows have
 177: `data_quality_valid=NULL`. They are treated as `NOT_TRUSTED`; they cannot make a
 178: new permissive decision: a bearish unknown 1h remains blocked, while a
 179: non-bearish unknown 1h retains its existing profile authority. The health view
 180: must expose `fix154.untrusted_bearish_1h_block_count` and
 181: `fix154.audit_write_failure_count`, `fix154.soft_capped_entry_count`, and
 182: `fix154.soft_add_blocked_count`, grouped by execution path.
 183: 
 184: ## Migration ordering and FIX-153 independence
 185: 
 186: The configured local database has V106 as its latest applied Flyway migration.
 187: Therefore FIX-154 reserves `V107__fix_154_trusted_higher_timeframe_buy.sql`.
 188: Before this migration is applied anywhere, rename the currently unreleased
 189: `V107__fix_153_local_candle_reconciliation.sql` to V108. Never rename or edit
 190: an applied migration. Do not enable Flyway `outOfOrder`; deployment order is
 191: made explicit instead.
 192: 
 193: ## Required regression suite
 194: 
 195: - All three integration paths use the shared service and no longer contain raw
 196:   `1H_BEARISH_VETO` branches.
 197: - `STRONG_SELL` + `confidence_score >= 70` and bearish fresh valid 5m produces
 198:   legacy `5M_BEARISH_VETO` with `HARD_CONFIRMATION_PRESENT` audit; the same 1h
 199:   with non-bearish 5m produces `HTF_TRUSTED_BEARISH_VETO`.
 200: - Strong/soft/untrusted 1h plus bullish, neutral, watch, bearish, missing,
 201:   stale, false-quality, and NULL-quality 5m covers every matrix row and its
 202:   final profile result.
 203: - `MISSING_1H_CONTEXT` (3h) and `MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION` (20m) retain their
 204:   priority and codes.
 205: - BNB #150a: persisted historical 1h bearish row with `NULL` quality =>
 206:   `NOT_TRUSTED` and `1H_BEARISH_VETO`.
 207: - BNB #150b: Replay regenerates that same 1h with quality evidence and it is
 208:   SOFT; no trusted hard veto is emitted and the final expected result is
 209:   `REJECTED_RISK_REWARD` when the risk/reward guard fails.
 210: - Historical `btcContextEntryAllowed=false` does not change usability; current
 211:   1m BTC behavior is unchanged.
 212: - FIX-154 migration succeeds with no `signal_mode` column and its assessment
 213:   has no FIX-153 schema/runtime dependency.
 214: - No future signal/candle is selected in Production or Replay; both produce
 215:   equivalent audit evidence for the same historical inputs.
 216: - Pre-migration `NULL` quality is never treated as valid.
 217: - Repeated execution/consolidation evaluations append audit rows and audit
 218:   failure leaves the business decision intact.
 219: - A SOFT initial entry never exceeds 25% through progressive, confirmation, or
 220:   accumulated-evidence or consolidation sizing; existing progressive raw-1h
 221:   support rules block add stages while the 1h remains bearish.
 222: 
 223: ## Release conditions
 224: 
 225: Run Java 21 unit/integration tests, migration tests, Production/Replay parity,
 226: and a bounded no-look-ahead historical replay before enabling FIX-154.
 227: FIX-153 remains independent and retains its own release gates.
 228: 
 229: ## Revision addendum ƒ?" Round 5 approval conditions
 230: 
 231: This addendum governs where it differs from earlier wording.
 232: 
 233: ### Release build decision
 234: 
 235: The FIX-154 release build includes `V108__fix_153_local_candle_reconciliation.sql`
 236: after the current unreleased FIX-153 V107 migration is renumbered to V108.
 237: This is required even with reconciliation disabled: `candle_analysis_outcome`
 238: is used by ordinary live signal processing, terminal worker outcomes, and the
 239: closed-candle detector. Omitting V108 would cause missing-table SQL failures;
 240: in particular, a normal signal-processing completion would roll back because
 241: its outcome write is inside the processing transaction.
 242: 
 243: Including V108 ships FIX-153 reconciliation *schema only*. The reconciliation
 244: runtime remains disabled and retains all of its separate release gates.
 245: 
 246: ### No FIX-153 provenance dependency
 247: 
 248: Ignore earlier references to historical-only status or signal modes in the
 249: FIX-154 band definition and audit payload. FIX-154 v1 eligibility depends only
 250: on time-bounded selection and `data_quality_valid = true`; `signal_mode` is not
 251: read, required, or stored by the FIX-154 audit.
 252: 
 253: ### Effective context is transient
 254: 
 255: The assessment returns immutable effective decision values separately from the
 256: loaded `TradeSignal` entities. No code may call `setDecision`, mutate, merge,
 257: or save a selected 1h/5m signal to obtain effective `NEUTRAL` context. Profile
 258: methods receive explicit effective decisions (or an immutable assessment
 259: context), while the raw entity remains audit evidence only. A regression test
 260: must load a SOFT 1h signal, execute the assessment/profile path, flush/clear,
 261: and prove its stored decision is unchanged.
 262: 
 263: ### Progressive-path evidence is observational
 264: 
 265: `progressivePositionDecision()` retains its existing raw-1h support check as
 266: the only decision authority for scout/add paths. If that existing check passes,
 267: FIX-154 performs no assessment lookup and writes no progressive audit row. If
 268: it rejects because raw 1h is bearish, a best-effort audit-only assessment may
 269: record `SOFT_HTF_ADD_BLOCKED` when the band is SOFT. That observation must not
 270: alter the rejection, allow an add, cause a database lookup on passing paths, or
 271: propagate an audit failure into the trading decision.
 272: 
 273: V108 ships with FIX-154 because the current runtime writes
 274: `candle_analysis_outcome`. Reconciliation remains disabled.
 275: 
 276: ### Round 5 text clarification
 277: 
 278: This addendum removes any earlier contrary implication: FIX-154 v1 has no
 279: `signal_mode` or historical-only predicate or audit field. Effective 1h/5m
 280: decisions are separate immutable values and never mutate a loaded signal.
 281: Progressive-path assessment is audit-only after its existing raw-1h check has
 282: already rejected; it is skipped entirely when that check passes and never feeds
 283: the scout/add decision.
 284: 
 285: ## Implementation completion record ƒ?" 2026-10-10
 286: 
 287: The approved FIX-154 design is now implemented in the working tree.
 288: 
 289: - `V107__fix_154_trusted_higher_timeframe_buy.sql` creates the nullable signal
 290:   quality evidence and append-only assessment audit table. No historical value
 291:   is fabricated or backfilled.
 292: - `AnalysisService` persists `MarketContextSnapshot.dataValid()` for every new
 293:   signal.
 294: - `HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService` owns the hard/soft/untrusted decision
 295:   matrix. It returns immutable effective decisions and does not mutate a JPA
 296:   signal.
 297: - Direct BUY validation, accumulated-evidence authority, and opportunity
 298:   consolidation invoke that one service. A SOFT result uses effective 1h
 299:   `NEUTRAL` and applies the 25% initial-position cap. The legacy bearish-5m
 300:   veto retains precedence. Progressive adds retain their existing raw 1h
 301:   requirement and can only create best-effort audit evidence after rejection.
 302: - `BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore` is append-only and runs in an independent
 303:   transaction. It catches insert failures so audit availability cannot alter a
 304:   business decision.
 305: 
 306: Focused Java 21 verification passed:
 307: `HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentServiceTest`,
 308: `TradeExecutionValidationServiceTest`, and
 309: `OpportunityConsolidationServiceTest`.
 310: 
 311: This is implementation completion, not production deployment approval. Apply
 312: V107/V108 only through the normal clean Java 21 release pipeline and complete
 313: the required migration, Replay-parity, and broader regression validation before
 314: enabling it in Production.

===== codex-docs/FIX-154_IMPLEMENTATION_HANDOFF.md =====
   1: # FIX-154 ƒ?" Implementation Handoff for Code Review
   2: 
   3: Date: 2026-10-10  
   4: Status: implemented in the local working tree; not deployed.
   5: 
   6: ## Scope
   7: 
   8: FIX-154 changes only higher-timeframe BUY authority. It does not enable or
   9: change FIX-153 reconciliation/runtime behavior.
  10: 
  11: The objective is to distinguish trusted, strong bearish 1h evidence from
  12: unknown or weaker evidence without changing a stored historical signal.
  13: 
  14: ## Implemented policy
  15: 
  16: 1. The current 1m BUY still requires fresh 5m and 1h context.
  17: 2. A bearish 5m returns the existing `5M_BEARISH_VETO` first.
  18: 3. A bearish 1h with `data_quality_valid` `NULL` or `false` returns
  19:    `1H_BEARISH_VETO`.
  20: 4. A trusted 1h `STRONG_SELL` with `confidence_score >= 70` returns
  21:    `HTF_TRUSTED_BEARISH_VETO`.
  22: 5. A trusted 1h `SELL`, or `STRONG_SELL` with confidence below 70, is a
  23:    SOFT conflict. It passes effective 1h `NEUTRAL` to profile logic and caps
  24:    every allowed initial entry at 25%.
  25: 6. A non-bearish 1h with unknown quality keeps its raw decision; FIX-154 does
  26:    not tighten bullish/non-bearish authority merely because historical quality
  27:    is absent.
  28: 7. Existing progressive/add logic retains its raw 1h requirement. A SOFT
  29:    conflict cannot authorize an add. Optional `SOFT_HTF_ADD_BLOCKED` evidence
  30:    is observational only.
  31: 
  32: ## Database changes
  33: 
  34: `V107__fix_154_trusted_higher_timeframe_buy.sql` adds:
  35: 
  36: - `trade_signal.data_quality_valid BOOLEAN NULL`.
  37: - Replay parity columns on `trade_signal_test`:
  38:   `btc_context_candle_close_time`, `btc_context_age_seconds`,
  39:   `btc_context_freshness_threshold_seconds`, `signal_mode`, and
  40:   `data_quality_valid`.
  41: - Append-only `buy_htf_assessment_audit`, including effective/raw decisions,
  42:   candle-close-based age, quality evidence, the hard-confirmation marker,
  43:   cap, legacy veto and final result.
  44: 
  45: Historical `data_quality_valid` stays `NULL`; no value is backfilled.
  46: 
  47: ## Included production code
  48: 
  49: | File | Change |
  50: |---|---|
  51: | `domain/TradeSignal.java` | Maps nullable `data_quality_valid`. |
  52: | `service/AnalysisService.java` | Persists `MarketContextSnapshot.dataValid()` at signal generation. |
  53: | `service/HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.java` | Pure FIX-154 decision matrix; returns detached effective decisions and never mutates entities. |
  54: | `service/TradeExecutionValidationService.java` | Applies assessment to direct BUY and contextual/accumulated authority; enforces SOFT cap; records non-blocking audit evidence. |
  55: | `service/OpportunityConsolidationService.java` | Applies the same matrix and cap to consolidation. |
  56: | `execution/service/ExecutionIntelligenceService.java` | Keeps existing progressive raw-1h guard; invokes optional audit-only SOFT evidence after its rejection. Uses `SETUP_WAKEUP` audit path for the 5m wake-up caller. |
  57: | `service/BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore.java` | Snapshots and asynchronously persists best-effort Production audit rows. Replay writes no Production audit row. |
  58: | `config/Fix154AuditAsyncConfig.java` | Dedicated one-thread, bounded executor; audit saturation drops evidence rather than borrowing a trading connection. |
  59: | `service/Fix154Metrics.java` | Non-gating counters for untrusted 1h blocks, audit failures, SOFT caps and SOFT add blocks. |
  60: | `service/Fix154DiagnosticsController.java` | Read-only `GET /api/fix154/health` metrics endpoint. |
  61: | `service/Fix154RuntimeSchemaChecks.java` | Checks V107-required production and Replay schema before runtime admission. |
  62: 
  63: ## Replay behavior
  64: 
  65: The regular reflection-based Replay writer persists every JPA `TradeSignal`
  66: column. V107 therefore updates `trade_signal_test` alongside production.
  67: FIX-154 audit rows are deliberately skipped while `ExecutionReplayScope` is
  68: active because replay-generated entities do not have Production IDs and parity
  69: is recorded by Replayƒ?Ts own evidence tables.
  70: 
  71: ## Tests included
  72: 
  73: - `HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentServiceTest`
  74:   - hard, soft, untrusted, 5m-veto precedence, 69/70 boundary, BNB #150a and
  75:     #150b policy cases, and unknown non-bearish authority.
  76: - `TradeExecutionValidationServiceTest`
  77:   - direct/context SOFT caps, conservative behavior, neutral-5m exception is
  78:     not unlocked, audit failure isolation, Production/Replay parity, and
  79:     future-context exclusion.
  80: - `OpportunityConsolidationServiceTest`
  81:   - SOFT cap through consolidation.
  82: - `BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStoreTest`
  83:   - Replay does not write a Production audit row.
  84: - `Fix154MigrationContractTest`
  85:   - V107 contains production, Replay and audit schema contracts.
  86: 
  87: ## Verification performed
  88: 
  89: Executed successfully with Java 21:
  90: 
  91: ```text
  92: mvn -q -Dtest=HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentServiceTest,
  93: TradeExecutionValidationServiceTest,
  94: OpportunityConsolidationServiceTest,
  95: BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStoreTest,
  96: Fix154MigrationContractTest test
  97: ```
  98: 
  99: ## Reviewer focus / remaining external proof
 100: 
 101: All FIX-154 implementation code described above is present in this local
 102: workspace. The remaining release proof is execution, not an identified code
 103: gap:
 104: 
 105: 1. Run Flyway V107 against a dedicated disposable MySQL schema and verify
 106:    `trade_signal`, `trade_signal_test`, and `buy_htf_assessment_audit`.
 107: 2. Run the full application/replay regression suite against that schema before
 108:    Production deployment.
 109: 3. Confirm `flyway_schema_history` on the target database has not already
 110:    applied V107; once applied, any correction must use a new forward migration.
 111: 
 112: No production database, service, or deployment configuration was changed by
 113: this implementation work.

 1684:     private TradeSignal recentAtrRetracementOrigin(TradeSignal current) {
 1685:         Instant cutoff = current.getGeneratedAt().minus(RETRACEMENT_SETUP_LOOKBACK);
 1686:         return recentSignals(current.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, current.getGeneratedAt()).stream()
 1687:                 .filter(s -> s != null && s.getGeneratedAt() != null)
 1688:                 .filter(s -> s.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(current.getGeneratedAt())
 1689:                         && !s.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(cutoff))
 1690:                 .filter(s -> isBullish(s.getOriginalDecision()) || isBullish(s.getDecision()))
 1691:                 .filter(s -> s.getTotalScore() >= RETRACEMENT_MIN_ORIGIN_SCORE
 1692:                         && s.getTrendScore() >= RETRACEMENT_MIN_ORIGIN_TREND
 1693:                         && s.getVolumeScore() >= RETRACEMENT_MIN_ORIGIN_VOLUME)
 1694:                 .filter(s -> !s.isAtrImmediateEntryAllowed() && s.isAtrOverextended())
 1695:                 .filter(s -> s.getAtrRetracementEntryPrice() != null && s.getAtrAtSignal() != null)
 1696:                 .filter(s -> s.isStrategyEntryAllowed() && s.isDerivativesEntryAllowed() && s.isLiquidityEntryAllowed())
 1697:                 .filter(s -> stopLossEvidence == null || stopLossEvidence.eligible(s, "DEFERRED_ORIGIN"))
 1698:                 .max(java.util.Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt))
 1699:                 .orElse(null);
 1700:     }

 1702:     private boolean isOneHourBearishWorsening(TradeSignal latest, TradeSignal current) {
 1703:         if (latest == null || current == null) return false;
 1704:         List<TradeSignal> hours = recentSignals(current.getSymbol(), TREND_INTERVAL, current.getGeneratedAt()).stream()
 1705:                 .filter(s -> s.getGeneratedAt() != null && s.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(latest.getGeneratedAt()))
 1706:                 .toList();
 1707:         TradeSignal previous = hours.isEmpty() ? null : hours.get(0);
 1708:         if (previous == null) return false;
 1709: 
 1710:         SignalDecision latestEffective = strongerDecision(latest.getDecision(), latest.getOriginalDecision());
 1711:         SignalDecision previousEffective = strongerDecision(previous.getDecision(), previous.getOriginalDecision());
 1712:         boolean severityWorsened = bearishSeverity(latestEffective) > bearishSeverity(previousEffective);
 1713:         return severityWorsened
 1714:                 || latest.getTotalScore() < previous.getTotalScore()
 1715:                 || latest.getTrendScore() < previous.getTrendScore()
 1716:                 || latest.getMomentumScore() < previous.getMomentumScore();
 1717:     }

 1719:     private int bearishSeverity(SignalDecision decision) {

 1721:         if (decision == SignalDecision.SELL) return 1;
 1722:         return 0;
 1723:     }

 1725:     private ExecutionDecision deferredContinuationDecision(TradeSignal current, Evidence e) {

 1732:                             + "already rejected the impulse (" + priceAction.summary() + "). Wait for fresh price "
 1733:                             + "support instead of chasing stale derived evidence.",
 1734:                     e);
 1735:         }

 1737:         // entry flag may be false solely because ATR requested a pullback; that is exactly

 1749:                 || five.getTotalScore() < DEFERRED_MIN_5M_SCORE
 1750:                 || five.getConfidenceScore() < DEFERRED_MIN_5M_CONFIDENCE) return null;

 1225:     /**
 1226:      * FIX-119 ƒ?" Accumulated-evidence late-entry timing deferral.
 1227:      *
 1228:      * ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE is opportunity memory. It may legitimately open a
 1229:      * position without a current BUY when persistent WATCH evidence has matured;
 1230:      * ICPUSDT #1702 is a proven example and must remain executable.
 1231:      *
 1232:      * The defect appears when that same WATCH-only authority reaches a price that
 1233:      * Entry Quality already classifies as LATE_ENTRY. In the current implementation,
 1234:      * scores 50-54 remain executable with reduced sizing, including cases where a
 1235:      * recent rejection-zone penalty was deliberately floored at the 50-point chase
 1236:      * cutoff. Historical BTCUSDT #1701, EDUUSDT #1716 and UNIUSDT #1731 demonstrate
 1237:      * that this can convert a valid opportunity into a poorly timed initial entry.
 1238:      *
 1239:      * FIX-119 therefore defers only when all of the following are simultaneously true:
 1240:      *   - the candidate is an allowed ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE initial entry;
 1241:      *   - Entry Quality is LATE_ENTRY;
 1242:      *   - accumulated fresh 1m evidence contains zero BUY observations; and
 1243:      *   - fresh 5m setup context is not BUY/STRONG_BUY.
 1244:      *
 1245:      * The result stays BUILDING rather than being rejected. Existing opportunity
 1246:      * evidence is preserved by saveOpportunity(), so subsequent signals can release
 1247:      * the opportunity when price geometry improves or fresh BUY authority appears.
 1248:      *
 1249:      * Good controls intentionally preserved:
 1250:      *   - ICPUSDT #1702: 0 BUY / 10 WATCH but ACCEPTABLE_ENTRY 55 -> unchanged.
 1251:      *   - SUIUSDT #1696: LATE_ENTRY 50 but accumulated BUY evidence and 5m BUY
 1252:      *     -> unchanged.
 1253:      *
 1254:      * A 1h BUY by itself does not bypass this guard. EDUUSDT #1716 had 1h BUY /
 1255:      * BALANCED_STRONG but remained a badly timed WATCH-only accumulated entry.

 1516:     private ExecutionDecision breakoutRetracementDecision(TradeSignal current, Evidence e) {
 1517:         if (current == null || current.getGeneratedAt() == null || current.getLatestPrice() == null) return null;
 1518:         if (isBearish(current.getDecision()) || isBearish(current.getOriginalDecision())) return null;
 1519:         if (!current.isAtrImmediateEntryAllowed() || !current.isFinalEntryAllowed()) return null;
 1520:         if (current.getTotalScore() < BREAKOUT_RETRACEMENT_MIN_CURRENT_SCORE

 1523:                 || current.getMomentumScore() < BREAKOUT_RETRACEMENT_MIN_CURRENT_MOMENTUM) return null;

 1533:         BigDecimal lower = target.subtract(atr.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(BREAKOUT_RETRACEMENT_MAX_OVERSHOOT_ATR)));

  397:     private SetupWakeupEvaluation evaluateConfirmedSetupWakeupInternal(
  398:             TradeSignal fiveMinuteTrigger, int currentAllocationPercent) {
  399:         if (currentAllocationPercent > 0 || fiveMinuteTrigger == null || fiveMinuteTrigger.getGeneratedAt() == null
  400:                 || !CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL.equals(fiveMinuteTrigger.getInterval())
  401:                 || !isBullish(fiveMinuteTrigger.getDecision())
  402:                 || !fiveMinuteTrigger.isFinalEntryAllowed()
  403:                 || !fiveMinuteTrigger.isAtrImmediateEntryAllowed()
  404:                 || !fiveMinuteTrigger.isLiquidityEntryAllowed()) {
  405:             return SetupWakeupEvaluation.none();
  406:         }
  407: 
  408:         TradeSignal previousFive = previousSignalBefore(
  409:                 fiveMinuteTrigger.getSymbol(), CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL, fiveMinuteTrigger.getGeneratedAt()).orElse(null);
  410:         if (previousFive != null && isBullish(previousFive.getDecision())) {
  411:             return SetupWakeupEvaluation.none();
  412:         }
  413: 
  414:         ExecutionOpportunity opportunity = currentOpportunity(
  415:                 fiveMinuteTrigger.getSymbol(), List.of("BUILDING", "WEAKENING", "BLOCKED", "CONFIRMED"))
  416:                 .filter(o -> o.getExecutedAt() == null)
  417:                 .orElse(null);
  418:         if (opportunity == null) return SetupWakeupEvaluation.none();
  419: 
  420:         TradeSignal current1m = latestSignalAtOrBefore(
  421:                 fiveMinuteTrigger.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, fiveMinuteTrigger.getGeneratedAt()).orElse(null);
  422:         if (current1m == null || current1m.getGeneratedAt() == null
  423:                 || Duration.between(current1m.getGeneratedAt(), fiveMinuteTrigger.getGeneratedAt())
  424:                         .compareTo(SETUP_WAKEUP_1M_MAX_AGE) > 0
  425:                 || !isSupportiveCurrentSignal(current1m)
  426:                 || !current1m.isFinalEntryAllowed()
  427:                 || !current1m.isAtrImmediateEntryAllowed()) {
  428:             return SetupWakeupEvaluation.none();
  429:         }
  430: 
  431:         HardRiskBlock hardBlock = nonAtrHardRiskBlock(current1m);
  432:         if (hardBlock.blocked()) return SetupWakeupEvaluation.none();
  433: 
  434:         TradeExecutionValidationService.ValidationResult authority =
  435:                 validationService.validateBuyContext(fiveMinuteTrigger, "SETUP_WAKEUP");
  436:         if (!authority.allowed()) return SetupWakeupEvaluation.none();
  437: 
  438:         Evidence evidence = evidence(current1m);
  439:         EntryQuality quality = assessEntryQuality(current1m, fiveMinuteTrigger.getAtrAtSignal());
  440:         int basePercent = Math.min(25, authority.positionPercent());
  441:         ExecutionDecision decision = applyInitialEntryQualityGuard(
  442:                 ExecutionDecision.allow(
  443:                         "SETUP_CONFIRMATION_WAKEUP",
  444:                         "FRESH_5M_CONFIRMATION",
  445:                         basePercent,
  446:                         "Fresh 5m BUY transition reactivated an existing unexecuted opportunity after the prior blocker cleared. "
  447:                                 + "Execution still uses latest 1m signal #" + current1m.getId()
  448:                                 + " at " + current1m.getLatestPrice() + "; 5m trigger #" + fiveMinuteTrigger.getId()
  449:                                 + " remains confirmation authority only. HTF profile=" + authority.code() + ".",
  450:                         evidence),
  451:                 quality);
  452:         if (!decision.allowed()) return new SetupWakeupEvaluation(current1m, decision);
  453: 
  454:         saveOpportunity(current1m, evidence, "CONFIRMED", decision.source(),
  455:                 decision.positionPercent(), decision.code(), decision.explanation());
  456:         return new SetupWakeupEvaluation(current1m, decision);
  457:     }

  459:     @Transactional
  460:     public ExecutionDecision evaluateBuy(TradeSignal signal, int currentAllocationPercent, String currentStage) {

  462:         return stopLossEvidence.evaluate(signal, currentAllocationPercent,
  463:                 () -> evaluateBuyInternal(signal, currentAllocationPercent, currentStage));
  464:     }
  465: 
  466:     private ExecutionDecision evaluateBuyInternal(TradeSignal signal, int currentAllocationPercent, String currentStage) {
  467:         if (signal == null || signal.getGeneratedAt() == null) {
  468:             return ExecutionDecision.reject("INVALID_SIGNAL", "Signal is missing required execution data.");
  469:         }
  470:         if (!EXECUTION_INTERVAL.equals(signal.getInterval())) {
  471:             return ExecutionDecision.observe("CONTEXT_ONLY", "Only fresh 1m signals may trigger a BUY execution.");
  472:         }
  473: 
  474:         if (isBearish(signal.getDecision())) {
  475:             Evidence evidence = evidence(signal);
  476:             if (isHardBearishReversal(signal, evidence)) {
  477:                 saveOpportunity(signal, evidence, "CANCELLED", "OPPORTUNITY_MEMORY", 0,
  478:                         "BEARISH_REVERSAL",
  479:                         "BUY opportunity cancelled because bearish evidence is strong or confirmed by 5m/1h context.");
  480:                 return ExecutionDecision.reject("BEARISH_REVERSAL",
  481:                         "Current bearish evidence is strong enough to invalidate the BUY opportunity.", evidence);
  482:             }
  483: 
  484:             if (evidence.opportunityHealth() < OPPORTUNITY_HEALTH_MIN_TO_KEEP) {
  485:                 saveOpportunity(signal, evidence, "CANCELLED", "OPPORTUNITY_MEMORY", 0,
  486:                         "OPPORTUNITY_HEALTH_EXHAUSTED",
  487:                         "BUY opportunity health fell below the minimum after bearish/aging penalties.");
  488:                 return ExecutionDecision.reject("OPPORTUNITY_HEALTH_EXHAUSTED",
  489:                         "Opportunity memory decayed below the safe minimum and was cancelled.", evidence);
  490:             }
  491: 
  492:             saveOpportunity(signal, evidence, "WEAKENING", "OPPORTUNITY_MEMORY", 0,
  493:                     "SOFT_BEARISH_INTERRUPTION",
  494:                     "A brief 1m bearish interruption reduced opportunity evidence instead of erasing it. "
  495:                             + "Health=" + evidence.opportunityHealth() + "/100, evidence=" + evidence.evidenceScore()
  496:                             + ". A new supportive signal is required before execution.");
  497:             return ExecutionDecision.weakening("SOFT_BEARISH_INTERRUPTION",
  498:                     "Bearish 1m evidence weakened the opportunity but did not invalidate supportive 5m/1h context.", evidence);
  499:         }
  500: 
  501:         // Non-ATR safety vetoes remain absolute except for one deliberately tiny,
  502:         // fully-auditable BTC-only exception. An exceptional independent-strength
  503:         // reversal may take a 10-15% probe when BTC is bearish, but only if the
  504:         // asset's own 5m/1h context is no longer bearish and every other hard gate
  505:         // plus ATR immediate-entry permission still passes.
  506:         HardRiskBlock nonAtrHardBlock = nonAtrHardRiskBlock(signal);
  507:         if (nonAtrHardBlock.blocked()) {
  508:             Evidence evidence = evidence(signal);
  509:             ExecutionDecision exceptionalProbe = exceptionalStrengthProbeDecision(signal, evidence, nonAtrHardBlock);
  510:             if (exceptionalProbe != null) {

 1885:     private ExecutionDecision pressureProbeDecision(TradeSignal current, Evidence e) {
 1886:         if (current == null || current.getGeneratedAt() == null) return null;
 1887: 
 1888:         // Preserve normal BUY authority. A bullish final decision belongs to the normal
 1889:         // path and must never fall back to this probe if normal validation rejects it.
 1890:         if (isBullish(current.getDecision()) || isBearish(current.getDecision())) return null;
 1891:         if (current.getDecision() != SignalDecision.WATCH && current.getDecision() != SignalDecision.NEUTRAL) return null;
 1892: 
 1893:         if (current.getTotalScore() < PRESSURE_PROBE_MIN_1M_SCORE
 1894:                 || current.getConfidenceScore() < PRESSURE_PROBE_MIN_1M_CONFIDENCE
 1895:                 || current.getTrendScore() < PRESSURE_PROBE_MIN_1M_TREND
 1896:                 || current.getVolumeScore() < PRESSURE_PROBE_MIN_1M_VOLUME
 1897:                 || current.getMomentumScore() < PRESSURE_PROBE_MIN_1M_MOMENTUM) return null;
 1898: 
 1899:         // These are existing production safety authorities; the pressure path cannot
 1900:         // manufacture permission when another context/risk layer already vetoed entry.
 1901:         if (!current.isFinalEntryAllowed()
 1902:                 || !current.isStrategyEntryAllowed()
 1903:                 || !current.isAtrImmediateEntryAllowed()
 1904:                 || !current.isBtcContextEntryAllowed()
 1905:                 || !current.isLiquidityEntryAllowed()
 1906:                 || !current.isDerivativesEntryAllowed()) return null;
 1907: 
 1908:         PressureReadinessService.Result pressure = pressureReadinessService.evaluate(current);
 1909:         if (pressure == null || !pressure.probeReady()) return null;
 1910: 
 1911:         TradeSignal latestFive = latestAtOrBefore(current, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL, FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE);
 1912:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(current, TREND_INTERVAL, ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE);
 1913:         if (latestFive == null || oneHour == null) return null;
 1914: 
 1915:         SignalDecision latestFiveDecision = strongerDecision(latestFive.getDecision(), latestFive.getOriginalDecision());
 1916:         SignalDecision oneHourDecision = strongerDecision(oneHour.getDecision(), oneHour.getOriginalDecision());
 1917: 
 1918:         // A fresh 5m STRONG_SELL is too hostile even for a probe. A plain 5m SELL can
 1919:         // represent the retest only when a recent 5m WATCH/BUY BREAKOUT already proved
 1920:         // the first bullish attempt. This is the SOL sequence: setup -> retest -> rebuild.
 1921:         if (latestFiveDecision == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL) return null;
 1922: 
 1923:         Instant setupCutoff = current.getGeneratedAt().minus(PRESSURE_RECENT_5M_SETUP_LOOKBACK);
 1924:         TradeSignal recentFiveMinuteSetup = recentSignals(current.getSymbol(), CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL, current.getGeneratedAt()).stream()
 1925:                 .filter(s -> s != null && s.getGeneratedAt() != null)
 1926:                 .filter(s -> !s.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(current.getGeneratedAt())
 1927:                         && !s.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(setupCutoff))
 1928:                 .filter(s -> {
 1929:                     SignalDecision d = strongerDecision(s.getDecision(), s.getOriginalDecision());
 1930:                     return d == SignalDecision.WATCH || isBullish(d);
 1931:                 })
 1932:                 .filter(s -> s.getSelectedStrategy() == TradingStrategy.BREAKOUT)
 1933:                 .filter(s -> s.getTotalScore() >= PRESSURE_PROBE_MIN_5M_SETUP_SCORE)
 1934:                 .filter(s -> s.getVolumeScore() >= PRESSURE_PROBE_MIN_5M_SETUP_VOLUME)
 1935:                 .filter(s -> s.getMomentumScore() >= PRESSURE_PROBE_MIN_5M_SETUP_MOMENTUM)
 1936:                 .findFirst()
 1937:                 .orElse(null);
 1938:         if (recentFiveMinuteSetup == null) return null;
 1939: 
 1940:         // The probe may coexist with a bearish 1h because it is explicitly counter-trend
 1941:         // discovery risk, not normal confirmation. We still require a fresh 1h object so
 1942:         // the conflict is known/auditable rather than silently treated as unavailable.
 1943:         if (oneHourDecision == null) return null;
 1944: 
 1945:         return ExecutionDecision.allow(
 1946:                 "PRESSURE_PROBE_ENTRY",
 1947:                 "ABSORPTION_RETEST_REBUILD_PROBE",
 1948:                 PRESSURE_PROBE_POSITION_PERCENT,
 1949:                 "Small pressure probe approved without changing the normal BUY path. "

 1781:                         + "Execution uses the current stop/target and a reduced " + percent + "% position.",

 1785:     private TradeSignal priorAtrDeferredBuy(TradeSignal current) {
 1786:         Instant cutoff = current.getGeneratedAt().minus(DEFERRED_BUY_LOOKBACK);
 1787:         return recentSignals(current.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, current.getGeneratedAt()).stream()
 1788:                 .filter(s -> s.getGeneratedAt() != null
 1789:                         && s.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(current.getGeneratedAt())
 1790:                         && !s.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(cutoff))
 1791:                 .filter(s -> isBullish(s.getDecision()) || isBullish(s.getOriginalDecision()))
 1792:                 .filter(s -> s.getTotalScore() >= properties.minimumBuyScore())
 1793:                 .filter(s -> !s.isAtrImmediateEntryAllowed())
 1794:                 .filter(s -> s.isStrategyEntryAllowed()
 1795:                         && s.isBtcContextEntryAllowed()
 1796:                         && s.isDerivativesEntryAllowed()
 1797:                         && s.isLiquidityEntryAllowed())
 1798:                 .filter(s -> {
 1799:                     String type = s.getAtrEntryType();
 1800:                     return "PULLBACK_ENTRY".equals(type) || "WAIT_FOR_RETRACEMENT".equals(type);
 1801:                 })
 1802:                 .filter(s -> stopLossEvidence == null || stopLossEvidence.eligible(s, "DEFERRED_ORIGIN"))
 1803:                 .findFirst()
 1804:                 .orElse(null);
 1805:     }

 1808:         return currentRewardRisk(signal, signal == null ? null : signal.getLatestPrice());
 1809:     }

 1811:     private double currentRewardRisk(TradeSignal signal, BigDecimal marketPrice) {
 1812:         if (signal == null || marketPrice == null || signal.getStopLoss() == null || signal.getTakeProfit() == null) return 0d;
 1813:         java.math.BigDecimal risk = marketPrice.subtract(signal.getStopLoss()).abs();
 1814:         java.math.BigDecimal reward = signal.getTakeProfit().subtract(marketPrice);
 1815:         if (risk.signum() <= 0 || reward.signum() <= 0) return 0d;
 1816:         return reward.divide(risk, 8, java.math.RoundingMode.HALF_UP).doubleValue();
 1817:     }

    1: package com.crypto.position.service;
    2: 
    3: import com.crypto.domain.SignalDecision;
    4: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
    5: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
    6: 
    7: /**
    8:  * Shared production/replay exit authority for an already-open long position.
    9:  *
   10:  * The policy deliberately separates short-timeframe noise from a genuine
   11:  * higher-timeframe breakdown:
   12:  *  - a 1m pullback alone never closes the position;
   13:  *  - the existing 1m SELL + bearish 5m confirmation remains valid;
   14:  *  - a fresh 1h SELL may close a position when 5m is no longer supportive;
   15:  *  - a Profit Lock breach is tolerated while 5m/1h still support the bullish thesis.
   16:  */
   17: @Component
   18: public class PositionExitPolicy {
   19: 
   20:     public Evaluation evaluateNormalExit(TradeSignal current, TradeSignal fiveMinute, TradeSignal oneHour) {
   21:         SignalDecision currentDecision = decision(current);
   22:         SignalDecision fiveDecision = decision(fiveMinute);
   23:         SignalDecision oneDecision = decision(oneHour);
   24: 
   25:         boolean oneHourBreakdown = bearish(oneDecision)
   26:                 && fiveMinute != null
   27:                 && !supportive(fiveDecision);
   28:         if (oneHourBreakdown) {
   29:             return Evaluation.exit("HTF_SELL_CONFIRMED",
   30:                     "1h turned " + label(oneDecision)
   31:                             + " while 5m is no longer supportive (" + label(fiveDecision)
   32:                             + "). The higher-timeframe bullish thesis is broken; close the managed position.");
   33:         }
   34: 
   35:         boolean lowerFrameConfirmed = current != null
   36:                 && "1m".equals(current.getInterval())
   37:                 && bearish(currentDecision)
   38:                 && fiveMinute != null
   39:                 && bearish(fiveDecision)
   40:                 && (oneHour == null || !bullish(oneDecision));
   41:         if (lowerFrameConfirmed) {
   42:             return Evaluation.exit("SELL_CONFIRMED",
   43:                     "1m " + label(currentDecision) + " is confirmed by bearish 5m while 1h is not bullish.");
   44:         }
   45: 
   46:         return Evaluation.hold("No confirmed multi-timeframe exit."
   47:                 + " current=" + label(currentDecision)
   48:                 + ", 5m=" + label(fiveDecision)
   49:                 + ", 1h=" + label(oneDecision) + ".");
   50:     }
   51: 
   52:     public Evaluation evaluateProfitLockBreach(TradeSignal current, TradeSignal fiveMinute, TradeSignal oneHour) {
   53:         SignalDecision currentDecision = decision(current);
   54:         SignalDecision fiveDecision = decision(fiveMinute);
   55:         SignalDecision oneDecision = decision(oneHour);
   56: 
   57:         // Strong HTF support: tolerate a sharp 1m pullback instead of throwing away the trend.
   58:         if (bullish(oneDecision) && fiveMinute != null && supportive(fiveDecision)) {
   59:             return Evaluation.hold("PROFIT_LOCK_HOLD",
   60:                     "Profit Lock was breached, but higher-timeframe continuation remains healthy:"
   61:                             + " 1h=" + label(oneDecision) + ", 5m=" + label(fiveDecision)
   62:                             + ", current=" + label(currentDecision)
   63:                             + ". Treat the breach as a protected pullback, not an immediate SELL.");
   64:         }
   65: 
   66:         // Cooling but still-supported trend: WATCH/WATCH is allowed to breathe while the current frame is not bearish.
   67:         if (oneDecision == SignalDecision.WATCH
   68:                 && fiveMinute != null && supportive(fiveDecision)
   69:                 && !bearish(currentDecision)) {
   70:             return Evaluation.hold("PROFIT_LOCK_HOLD",
   71:                     "Profit Lock was breached during a supported consolidation:"
   72:                             + " 1h=WATCH, 5m=" + label(fiveDecision)
   73:                             + ", current=" + label(currentDecision)
   74:                             + ". Keep the winner protected while waiting for higher-timeframe confirmation.");
   75:         }

  477:             BigDecimal executionPrice,
  478:             String executionReason,
  479:             String executionMessage
  480:     ) {
  481:         String pair = normalizePair(symbol);
  482:         if(walletCoordination!=null) { walletCoordination.symbol(pair); }
  483:         WalletManagedPosition position = managedPositionRepository
  484:                 .findFirstBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(pair, "OPEN")
  485:                 .orElse(null);
  486:         if(walletCoordination!=null)walletCoordination.mutation();
  487:         // FIX-125: position precedes mutation; statistics precede cash on every exit.
  488:         dailyStatisticsRepository.findForUpdateByTradeDate(LocalDate.now(ZoneOffset.UTC));
  489: 
  490:         if (position == null || position.getId() == null || position.getQuantity() == null
  491:                 || position.getQuantity().signum() <= 0) {
  492:             return false;
  493:         }
  494: 
  495:         String reason = executionReason == null || executionReason.isBlank()
  496:                 ? "MECHANICAL_EXIT"
  497:                 : executionReason.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
  498:         String key = "POSITION:" + position.getId() + ":" + reason;
  499:         if (executionExists(key)) return true;
  500: 
  501:         String assetSymbol = pair.substring(0, pair.length() - 4);
  502:         if(walletCoordination!=null)getOrCreate("USDT");
  503:         WalletAsset coin = getOrCreate(assetSymbol);
  504:         BigDecimal quantity = position.getQuantity().min(coin.getQuantity());
  505:         if (quantity.signum() <= 0) return false;
  506: 
  507:         BigDecimal price = positive(executionPrice);
  508:         BigDecimal gross = quantity.multiply(price);
  509:         BigDecimal costBasis = position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt().multiply(quantity);
  510:         BigDecimal realized = gross.subtract(costBasis);
  511:         BigDecimal realizedPercent = costBasis.signum() == 0 ? ZERO : realized
  512:                 .divide(costBasis, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP)
  513:                 .multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100));
  514: 
  515:         coin.setQuantity(coin.getQuantity().subtract(quantity));
  516:         if (coin.getQuantity().signum() == 0) coin.setAverageBuyPriceUsdt(null);
  517:         assetRepository.save(coin);
  518:         // FIX-037: credit SELL proceeds atomically so a concurrent BUY cannot overwrite them.
  519:         if (assetRepository.creditQuantity("USDT", gross) != 1) {
  520:             throw new IllegalStateException("Unable to credit USDT wallet balance");
  521:         }
  522:         // FIX-037: keep a final post-credit balance snapshot for lambda-based daily-stat updates.
  523:         // Using a separate final variable also avoids capturing a reassigned local variable.
  524:         final BigDecimal endingUsdt = getOrCreate("USDT").getQuantity();
  525: 
  526:         position.setQuantity(ZERO);
  527:         position.setTotalCostUsdt(ZERO);
  528:         position.setAverageEntryPriceUsdt(ZERO);
  529:         position.setStatus("CLOSED");
  530:         position.setUpdatedAt(Instant.now());
  531:         managedPositionRepository.save(position);
  532: 
  533:         tradeRepository.save(WalletTrade.builder()
  534:                 .executionKey(key)
  535:                 .symbol(pair)
  536:                 .side("SELL")
  537:                 .quantity(quantity)
  538:                 .priceUsdt(price)
  539:                 .grossAmountUsdt(gross)
  540:                 .feeUsdt(ZERO)
  541:                 .netAmountUsdt(gross)
  542:                 .costBasisUsdt(costBasis)
  543:                 .realizedPnlUsdt(realized)
  544:                 .realizedPnlPercent(realizedPercent)
  545:                 .executionType("PAPER_AUTO")
  546:                 .executionReason(reason)
  547:                 .status("EXECUTED")
  548:                 .executedAt(Instant.now())
  549:                 .notes("Mechanical live-price position exit")
  550:                 .executionMessage(executionMessage == null ? reason + " triggered at " + price : executionMessage)
  551:                 .build());
  552: 
  553:         dailyStatisticsRepository.findForUpdateByTradeDate(LocalDate.now(ZoneOffset.UTC))
  554:                 .ifPresent(daily -> {
  555:                     daily.setEndingUsdt(endingUsdt);
  556:                     daily.setEndingPortfolioUsdt(walletService.currentPortfolioValue());
  557:                     daily.setUpdatedAt(Instant.now());
  558:                     dailyStatisticsRepository.save(daily);
  559:                 });
  560:         walletService.captureSnapshot();

  431: 
  432:         return completeClose(position, exitPrice,
  433:                 exitPrice.compareTo(position.getStopLoss()) <= 0 ? PositionStatus.STOPPED : PositionStatus.CLOSED,
  434:                 "MANUAL_CLOSE", "Position was manually closed.", null);
  435:     }

  442:     private PaperPosition closeFromSignal(
  443:             PaperPosition position,
  444:             TradeSignal signal,
  445:             PositionStatus status,
  446:             String closeReason,
  447:             String explanation

  451: 
  452:     private PaperPosition closeFromProfitLock(
  453:             PaperPosition position,
  454:             TradeSignal signal,
  455:             DynamicProfitLockService.Evaluation profitLock
  456:     ) {
  457:         BigDecimal exitPrice = signal.getLatestPrice();
  458:         BigDecimal remainingPnl = exitPrice.subtract(position.getEntryPrice(), MC)
  459:                 .multiply(position.getQuantity(), MC);
  460:         // FIX-11T: preserve any P&L already realized by a Near-TP partial harvest.
  461:         BigDecimal pnl = (position.getRealizedPnl() == null ? BigDecimal.ZERO : position.getRealizedPnl())
  462:                 .add(remainingPnl, MC);
  463: 
  464:         position.setExitPrice(exitPrice);
  465:         position.setRealizedPnl(pnl);
  466:         position.setStatus(PositionStatus.CLOSED);
  467:         position.setCloseReason("PROFIT_LOCK");
  468:         position.setExitReason(profitLock.explanation());
  469:         position.setExitSignal(signal);
  470:         position.setClosedAt(Instant.now());
  471:         PaperPosition saved = positionRepository.save(position);
  472: 
  473:         // FIX-020: terminal position close consumes the opportunity that financed this
  474:         // position so old pre-exit evidence cannot immediately open a brand-new trade.
  475:         executionIntelligenceService.completePositionOpportunity(position.getSymbol(), signal, "PROFIT_LOCK");
  476: 
  477:         try {
  478:             walletAutoExecutionService.executeProfitLock(signal, exitPrice, profitLock.lockPrice());
  479:         } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
  480:             log.error("Dynamic Profit Lock wallet exit failed for signal {}: {}", signal.getId(), ex.getMessage(), ex);
  481:         }
  482:         // FIX-028: profit-lock exits use the same immutable production audit trail.
  483:         productionExitAuditService.record(saved, signal, "PROFIT_LOCK", profitLock.explanation());
  484:         return saved;
  485:     }
  486: 
  487:     private PaperPosition completeClose(
  488:             PaperPosition position,
  489:             BigDecimal exitPrice,
  490:             PositionStatus status,
  491:             String closeReason,
  492:             String explanation,
  493:             TradeSignal exitSignal
  494:     ) {
  495:         BigDecimal remainingPnl = exitPrice.subtract(position.getEntryPrice(), MC)
  496:                 .multiply(position.getQuantity(), MC);
  497:         // FIX-11T: preserve any P&L already realized by a Near-TP partial harvest.
  498:         BigDecimal pnl = (position.getRealizedPnl() == null ? BigDecimal.ZERO : position.getRealizedPnl())
  499:                 .add(remainingPnl, MC);
  500: 
  501:         position.setExitPrice(exitPrice);
  502:         position.setRealizedPnl(pnl);
  503:         position.setStatus(status);
  504:         position.setCloseReason(closeReason);
  505:         position.setExitReason(explanation);
  506:         position.setExitSignal(exitSignal);
  507:         position.setClosedAt(Instant.now());
  508:         PaperPosition saved = positionRepository.save(position);
  509: 
  510:         // FIX-020: all terminal exits (TP/SL/SIGNAL_SELL/manual/etc.) create an immutable
  511:         // evidence boundary. Progressive adds remain untouched while the position is open.
  512:         executionIntelligenceService.completePositionOpportunity(position.getSymbol(), exitSignal, closeReason);
  513: 
  514:         if (exitSignal != null) {
  515:             try {
  516:                 // FIX-028: keep execution behavior identical, but preserve the TRUE terminal
  517:                 // reason in wallet history. A TAKE_PROFIT/STOP_LOSS may use the latest 1m
  518:                 // signal as its price/context carrier even when that signal is WATCH. Calling
  519:                 // executeSell(...) used to mislabel those exits as SIGNAL_SELL.
  520:                 if ("SELL".equalsIgnoreCase(closeReason) || "STRONG_SELL".equalsIgnoreCase(closeReason)) {
  521:                     walletAutoExecutionService.executeSell(exitSignal);
  522:                 } else {
  523:                     walletAutoExecutionService.executeSignalLinkedExit(exitSignal, closeReason, explanation);
  524:                 }
  525:             } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
  526:                 log.error("Automatic wallet exit failed for signal {}: {}", exitSignal.getId(), ex.getMessage(), ex);
  527:             }
  528:         }
  529: 
  530:         // FIX-028: immutable production audit records the actual close trigger separately
  531:         // from the latest market signal. This is diagnostic only and cannot alter execution.
  532:         productionExitAuditService.record(saved, exitSignal, closeReason, explanation);
  533:         return saved;
  534:     }

  538:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Symbol is required");
  539:         }
  540:         return symbol.trim().toUpperCase();
